    function normalizePlungerModelSlug(value: unknown) {
      return cleanPlungerHrefText(value)
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/μ/g, "u")
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
        .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
    }
